# Attributes
Aliases: Atributes, Attribute, Attribute Shard, Attribute Shards, Combat Shard, Combat Shards, Combat shard, Combat shards, Farming Attributes, Farming Shards, Farming attributes, Farming shards, Fishing Attributes, Fishing Shards, Fishing attributes, Fishing shards, Hunting Box, Hunting Shard, Hunting Shards, Hunting shard, Hunting shards, Mining Attribute, Mining Attributes, Mining Shard, Mining Shards, Mining attribute, Mining attributes, Mining shard, Mining shards, Shard, Shards
Categories: Pages with UIs, More info needed, Mechanics
[Attributes]
Category: Mechanics
Attributes are special passive abilities and buffs typically obtained through [[Hunting]]. Each Attribute has a corresponding Attribute Shard, a type of item that is automatically stored in the player's Hunting Box when obtained. These can also be fused into other shards, or syphoned into their respective attribute, with more syphons increasing the attribute's level, up to level 10.
## Obtaining
Attribute Shards are obtained in multiple ways:
- [[Hunting]] mobs with [[Critter Capsule|Critter Capsules]], [[Fishing Nets]], [[Lassos]], or [[Black Holes]].
- Killing mobs that have a shard with [[Charm Chance]].
- Dropping from certain [[Bosses]], [[Slayer|SLAYER BOSSES]], [[Minibosses]], [[Minibosses|SLAYER MINIBOSSES]]
- Using [[Huntraps]] in certain locations.
- Fusing with the [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion Machine]].
- Reward from [[Tree Gifts]].
- Rewards chests in [[Kuudra]] and [[Dungeons]].
- Purchased from [[Agatha]], [[Miria]], [[Kiara]] and [[Sanger]].
- Crafting Recipes
Attributes can be turned on or off in the Attribute menu.
### Attribute Fusion
5 Attributes can be fused to create new Attributes. Reptilian, Elemental, and Amphibian Attributes only require 2 to fuse. The resulting fusions can be previewed before confirming. Both sets of Attributes are consumed in the process.
## Requirements
Most Attributes require the following [[Hunting]] levels to Syphon:
- [[Rarity|COMMON]] – 0
- [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] – 5
- [[Rarity|RARE]] – 10
- [[Rarity|EPIC]] – 15
- [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] – 20
Attributes that existed prior to [[Hunting]] have no requirement, except for Attack Speed ( [[Inferno Demonlord Shard]]), which has the standard [[Hunting|Hunting XV (15)]] requirement.
## Hunting Box
The Hunting Box is a menu that stores Attribute Shards. It can be accessed from the [[Hunting]] Skill menu or from the /huntingbox command. Any Attribute Shards that are collected automatically get sent to the Hunting Box.
While in the Hunting Box, Attribute Shards can be syphoned into an attribute, or [[Attribute Fusion|Fused]] into a new attribute. The box can be sorted by ID and newest. Shards can also be right-clicked to be taken out from the box and turned into an item.
Clicking the chest at the top of the menu allows the player to swap from the Hunting Box to the Attribute Menu.
## Leveling
Attribute Shards can be syphoned to the Hunting Box by pressing Right-Click while holding them. Pressing Left-Click an Attribute in the Hunting Box syphons it and contributes towards leveling up its respective Attribute.
Attributes begin at Level Ⅰ (1) and can be upgraded up to Level Ⅹ (10) by syphoning more shards. Higher [[Rarity]] Attributes require less shards to increase their levels. The amount of Attribute Shards required to reach max level for each rarity of an Attribute is as follows:
|Level|COMMON Shards|COMMON Cumulative|UNCOMMON Shards|UNCOMMON Cumulative|RARE Shards|RARE Cumulative|EPIC Shards|EPIC Cumulative|LEGENDARY Shards|LEGENDARY Cumulative|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
[1]
|2|3|4|2|3|2|3|1|2|1|2|
|3|5|9|3|6|3|6|2|4|1|3|
|4|6|15|4|10|3|9|2|6|2|5|
|5|7|22|5|15|4|13|3|9|2|7|
|6|8|30|6|21|4|17|3|12|2|9|
|7|10|40|7|28|5|22|4|16|3|12|

|9|18|72|12|48|8|36|5|25|4|19|

## Tips
- Regarding [[Silentdepth Shard]] and [[Thyst Shard]]:
  - Because their hunting methods overlap in the [[Jungle]], it is recommended to place [[Huntraps]] in one of the other 3 quadrants.
  - Reliable bodies of water can be found :
    - in the [[Lost Precursor City]]
    - in the [[Goblin Holdout]] at [[Coordinates|361, 166, 526]].
  - Alternatively, one can use [[Wooden Bait]] in the [[Crystal Hollows]] to guarantee reeling in [[Silentdepth Shard]](s) as their next [[Treasure|GOOD CATCH]].
## Bugs
- [[Thyst Shard]]’s hunting area remains extended across all 4 quadrants despite its description. Thus, [[Silentdepth Shard]] cannot be hunted specifically from [[Huntraps]].
  - A secure alternative is to use [[Wooden Bait|Wooden Baits]].
- [[Wisp Pet|Wisp Pets]] are affected by the Battle Experience attribute when being fed [[Gabagool (disambiguation)|Gabagools]], despite it not being from Combat.
## Trivia
- Prior to 0.23, Attributes were bound to items. Items could be fused together at [[Igor]] or [[Alwin]] to increase their level.
- Attribute Shards generally follow the Forest, Water, Combat pattern when ordered by ID. Gaps in IDs likely exist as a reservation for future shards of the corresponding category.
  - [[Fenlord Shard]] (E7) used to be Water instead of the expected Forest, but this was changed.
  - [[Starborn Shard]] (L44) used to be Combat instead of the expected Water, but this was changed.
- [[Bezal Shard]] (Blazing Resistance)'s head icon is an upside-down version of the [[Plushie Blaze Skin]].
- [[Naga Shard]] (Charmed) head icon is Duke Fishron from Terraria.
- Lifeline ( [[Kada Knight Shard]]) may be based off a League of Legends passive of the same name, with similar activation requirements and effects.
- Unlimited Power ( [[Jormung Shard]]) may be a reference to Star Wars, specifically a quote in Revenge of the Sith by Darth Sidious.
- Vitality ( [[Kraken Shard]]) internal name is mending as it used to be called that way.
- Prior to 0.24.5, the [[Hellwisp Shard]] and the [[Inferno Demonlord Shard]] (then known as [[Inferno Demonlord Shard|Burningsoul Shard]]) had each other's IDs and rarities. When the update dropped, all previous instances of either shards syphoned were swapped, but unsyphoned shards were unchanged. A player who had Level 10 Matriarch Cubs Attribute prior to the update will instead have Level 10 Attack Speed Attribute instead.
  - This change was made to ensure players did not need to re-hunt Attributes they had already maxed out. However, 0.27 changed the [[Thorn Shard]] and [[Scarf Shard]] from [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] to [[Rarity|EPIC]], breaking this consistency.
- Changelog from 0.24.5 is mistaken as Crystal Serendipity ( [[Silentdepth Shard]]) has been functional on lock-pickable chests since 0.24.1.
- As of 0.27, [[Bitbug Shard]] cannot be fused into via [[Chameleon Shard]], and [[Grizzly Bear Shard]] cannot be fused with [[Chameleon Shard]].
  - This is likely to prevent progression skipping, as otherwise one would be able to easily acquire [[Molthorn Shard]] for extremely cheap.
- The [[Rainbug Shard]] is the only shard that has its method of unlocking obfuscated in the menu.

# Berserk
Aliases: Berserker, Berzerk, Beserk
Categories: Pages with UIs, Dungeons, Classes
The Berserk Class's Dungeon Orb
Berserk is a [[Classes|Class]] in the [[Catacombs]]. It primarily focuses on substantial damage targeted at close-range mobs.
## Class Passives
|Name|Description|
|-|-|
|Bloodlust|Your next hit after killing a monster deals increased damage, expiring after 5s. On activation, the cooldown on Throwing Axe is reduced by 1s. The player heals 3% of their missing [[Health]] every hit.|
|Lust For Blood|Every time you hit an enemy, increases the maximum damage you deal to an enemy, up to a certain threshold.|
|Indomitable|Gain a percentage of your [[Strength]] as [[Defense]].|
|Weapon Master|Your melee weapons hit up to 5 enemies within your [[Swing Range]] in a frontal cone. Increases your maximum [[Swing Range]].|
## Dungeon Orb Abilities
- Throwing Axe: Throw an Axe, doing the damage of your highest damage hit in the last minute. Cooldown: 10 seconds
- Ragnarok: Grants 100 [[Attack Speed]], 400 [[Speed]], and a 50% damage increase for 15 seconds. Also summons 3 Zombie minions to aid you in battle! Cooldown: 60 seconds
## Ghost Abilities
- Buff Potion: Throw a potion which temporarily gives all [[Dungeons|teammates]] in a 10 block radius +30 [[Strength]]
- Ghost Axe: Throw an Axe, dealing damage based on your berserker level!
## Leveling Rewards
For every Berserk level up to level 50 you get:
- + 0.75% Melee Damage
- + 0.4% Bloodlust Damage
- + 1% Throwing Axe Damage
- + 50-1000 Ghost Axe Damage
- + 800-16000 Ragnarok Minions Health
- + 3% Lust for Blood Damage Increase Per Hit
For every 5 Berserk Levels you also get:
- + 0.4 [[Speed]]
- + 2 Ghost Potion [[Strength]]
- + 100% Lust for Blood Damage Increase Cap
At level 50 you receive the following buffs:
- +37.5% Melee Damage, for a total of +77.5% Melee Damage
- +20% Bloodlust Damage, for a total of +40%
- +50% Throwing axe Damage
- +4 [[Speed]], for a total of +34 [[Speed]]
- +20 Ghost Potion [[Strength]], for a total of +50 [[Strength]]
- +1000% Lust for Blood Damage Increase Cap, for a total of 1100%
## Milestones
Berserk class can reach its milestones by dealing melee [[Damage]] in the dungeon. This table [[Class Milestones|showcases class milestones]].
## Gallery
- One of the zombies summoned when using Ragnarok.

# Bingo
Aliases: Bingo Event, Bingo Point, Bingo Points, Bingo Rank, Bingo rank, Extreme Bingo, Secret Bingo, Secret Bingo Event
Categories: Pages with UIs, Outdated pages, Events, Bingo
[Bingo]
Bingo NPC
Bingo is a gamemode playable during the monthly SkyBlock Bingo event. The gamemode is accessible through [[Bingo (NPC)|Bingo]] for the first 7 days of every real life month, and is available to everyone with [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock Level 10]] or above.
Players who participate are challenged to reach certain milestones (solo and community goals) on a new profile to gain Bingo Points. These points can be used in the [[Bingo (NPC)|Bingo Shop]] to purchase various items.
## Overview
To participate, players must create a temporary Bingo profile to fill the Bingo Card. Bingo profiles occupy a dedicated slot so there is no need to delete a profile, if the profile limit is reached, to participate.
[[Auction House]], [[Bazaar]], [[Museum]], [[SkyBlock Gems|Gem Shop]] and [[Trading]] are disabled on Bingo profiles.
Next to the [[Bingo (NPC)|Bingo]], you will find [[Alixer]] who will grant you with potion effects, which increase with each Ⓑ Bingo Rank. After completing four personal goals, you can add [[Alixer]] to your [[Abiphones|AⒷiphone]] contacts to receive potions from anywhere!
## Interface
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
### Bingo Card
  See #Tasks for more information about tasks.
Players can complete goals, which are milestones or tasks. Completing them awards the player with Bingo Points. Additional points can be awarded for completing every goal in a row, column, or diagonal.
The card consists of 25 goals in a 5x5 grid. Twenty of them are Personal Goals, and five are Community Goals, which can be contributed to by any participant. When in the Top 100 contributors for a Community Goal, the player's contribution rank is displayed.
Completing every Personal Goal awards the player with a [[Bingo Card (Item)|Bingo Card]]. This can be claimed on any profile from [[Jerry]].
## Tasks
See current and previous Bingo Cards in detail at [[Bingo/Events]].
### Tasks Distribution
In Bingo, there are two types of goals: Personal Goals and Community Goals.
Personal Goals:
- Personal goals are completed per person.
- Each personal goal has a hidden "difficulty level", ranging from 1 to 5.
- Each row/column on the Bingo Card has four personal goals, with a total level ranging from 8 to 13.
- Some personal goals have assigned "groups". No more than 1 goal of a group can exist on one Bingo Card.
- "Collector Goal" is a type of personal goals. A maximum of three collection goals can be present on the card.
Community Goals:
- Community goals are contributed by everyone playing bingo.
- Community goals are on the main diagonal of the Bingo Card, one on each row/column.
- Community goals are separated in "tiers", ranging from Tier I (1) to Tier V (5). They start from Tier I at the beginning of the event.
- When completing a tier, the next tier, with the required values of those of Tier I multiplied by the tier number, will be available to achieve.
### All Available Tasks

You can help the wiki by editing this page and reworking it.

### Bingo Rank
Bingo Points allow the player to purchase a Ⓑ Bingo Rank. Some items in the [[Bingo (NPC)|Bingo Shop]] have a Ⓑ Bingo Rank requirement. Each Ⓑ Bingo Rank upgrade also gives the player better perks during the event. A Ⓑ Bingo Rank is permanent once purchased.
- Ⓑ Bingo Rank I (1) is obtained by purchasing upgrade using 50 Bingo Points.
- Ⓑ Bingo Rank II (2) is obtained by purchasing upgrade using 100 Bingo Points.
- Ⓑ Bingo Rank III (3) is obtained by purchasing upgrade using 150 Bingo Points.
- Ⓑ Bingo Rank IV (4) is obtained by purchasing upgrade using 200 Bingo Points.
Bingo players gain the following perks depending on their Ⓑ Bingo Rank. Perks from previous ranks are included.
|Perks|Ⓑ No Bingo Rank|Ⓑ Bingo Rank I (1)|Ⓑ Bingo Rank II (2)|Ⓑ Bingo Rank III (3)|Ⓑ Bingo Rank IV (4)|

|5 Medium Storages ([[Minion Storage]])|✔|✔|✔|✔|✔|
|1 AⒷiphone ([[Abiphones]]) with [[Maddox the Slayer]]'s contact|✔|✔|✔|✔|✔|
|+30% Skill ([[Skills]]) Experience|✔|✔|✔|✔|✔|
|+20 [[Magic Find]]|✔|✔|✔|✔|✔|
|Maximum [[Enchanting]] Power in the Hub ([[Hub Island]])|✔|✔|✔|✔|✔|
|[[Personal Bank]] is unlocked|✔|✔|✔|✔|✔|

### Unlockable Items
There are several Extreme and Secret Bingo exclusive items that can be unlocked by completing a specific number of goals:
- [[Bingo Wisdom Talisman]]
- [[Bingonimbus 2000]]
- [[Bingolibur]]
- [[Binghoe]]
- [[Bingbow]]
- [[Bingo Rod]]
- [[Bingo Lava Rod]]
- [[Bingo Booster]]
- [[Bingo Rift Talisman]]
- [[Bingo Blaster]]
These are all [[Soulbound]] and cannot be transferred to, or obtained on, other profiles.
### Item Transfer
Up to 10 slots worth of Items (this includes Stackables up to their maximum Stack (1/16/64)) on a Bingo profile, excluding [[Soulbound]] items, can be transferred to a regular profile or an [[Ironman]] profile.
The items can be claimed on the receiving profile from [[Jerry]] on your private island.
[[Bingo (NPC)|Bingo]] remains in the [[Village]] for three days following the conclusion of the event. This gives you time to transfer items before the Bingo profile is permanently deleted, or to make purchases in the Bingo Shop.
## Bingo Modes
### Extreme Bingo
The Extreme Bingo Event mode is a harder version of Ⓑ Bingo that occurs with an admin's notice. When it occurs, it replaces the normal Bingo Event of that month.
Extreme Bingo lasts for 14 Earth days instead of 7, features tasks of increased difficulty, and doubles the value of Bingo Points earned. A special [[Bingo Card (Item)|Bingo Card]] will be rewarded if all tasks are completed.
### Secret Bingo
Similar to Extreme Bingo, Secret Bingo is a modified version of Bingo that occurs with an admin's notice. When it occurs, it replaces the normal Bingo Event of that month. Secret Bingo lasts for 14 Earth days instead of 7 and doubles the value of Bingo Points earned. Additionally, all personal and community goal objectives are hidden until discovered. Personal goal titles are not revealed until the beginning of the event, with up to three hints being revealed over the first seven days for each goal if not discovered. When a personal goal is completed, the player's position in how fast they completed the goal compared to other players is revealed. A special [[Bingo Card (Item)|Bingo Card]] is rewarded if all tasks are completed. [[Bingo's Secrets]] is also obtained by the first player to complete each personal goal.
### Obtaining
|Goal Type|Goal Method|Goal Notes|Bingo Points|
|-|-|-|-|
|Personal|Completing a "Personal Goal".|Personal Goals can only be completed by the player who receives them.|1|
|Community Goal|Contributing to a "Community Goal".|Once a Goal is completed, the Community Goal will level up to the next tier.; Community Goals can be contributed to by anyone.; The amount of Bingo Points received by players depends on their contributions to the goal and the tier of the goal.; A goal reaching position #100 or higher will provide Bingo Points for the top 1%.|Tiers|
|Community Goal|Contributing to a "Community Goal".|(same as above)|1 2 3 4 5|
|Community Goal|Contributing to a "Community Goal".|(same as above)|Top 1%: 5; Top 5%: 4; Top 10%: 3; Top 25%: 2; All: 1 Top 1%: 7; Top 5%: 5; Top 10%: 4; Top 25%: 3; All: 1 Top 1%: 9; Top 5%: 7; Top 10%: 5; Top 25%: 3; All: 1 Top 1%: 12; Top 5%: 10; Top 10%: 7; Top 25%: 5; All: 2 Top 1%: 15; Top 5%: 12; Top 10%: 9; Top 25%: 7; All: 4|
|Row|Completing a row or column of Goals.||5|
|Column|Completing a row or column of Goals.||5|
|Community Diagonal|Completing all "Community Goals".|The column of "Community Goals" is from bottom right to top left.|5|
|Diagonal|Completing a diagonal of "Personal Goals".||10|
## Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Event Starts|----------------------------------------------------- The BINGO Event is starting! Complete Bingo tasks to earn Bingo Points! You can start a bingo profile by $[ctalking to Bingo in the hub.CLICK HERE to warp to the hub! -----------------------------------------------------|
|Creating a Bingo Profile|----------------------------------------------------- Welcome to SkyBlock Bingo! [Month] [Year] This is your Bingo island! Complete the goals on this month's Bingo Card to earn Bingo Points! Any Bingo Points that you earn can be spent in the Bingo Shop on your other profiles. At the end of the event, your Bingo island and profile will be deleted! Good luck! -----------------------------------------------------|
|Event Ends|----------------------------------------------------- SkyBlock Bingo - [Month] [Year] Community Goals <All community goals and completions> You weren't awarded any bonus Bingo Points for Community Goal contributions. -----------------------------------------------------|
## Bugs
- In the 3rd event (February 2022), community goal with contributions in the position of #100 or higher gives only "All Contributors Reward" rather than the right amount regarding to the percentile. Many screenshots of wrongly-calculated rewards can be found in the forum thread.
  - The lacking bingo points have been complemented in the start of 4th event (March 2022).
- During the 10th event (September 2022), brewing stands were disabled making it harder to get a 17 Skill Average for the "Skill Average" task. On the last day of the Bingo event the Skill Average requirement was lowered from 17 to 14 to make up for the fact that players could not level their alchemy using brewing stands.
## Trivia
- Despite the Community Shop being disabled, account upgrades (e.g. [[Accessory Bag]] upgrade) still take effect.
- Upgrading your Bingo Rank in the middle of a Bingo event will give you access to better potion effects from [[Alixer]], but won't give you other perks such as an upgraded rarity [[Bingo Pet]].
- The highest amount of points obtainable during a single Bingo event is 160: 20 from personal goals, up to 75 from community tiers, 50 from rows and columns, 5 from the community diagonal and 10 from the personal diagonal.
- 2024 is the first year to have no Extreme or Secret Bingo.

# Bits Shop
Categories: Pages with UIs, Bits Shop, Shops
The Bits Shop, accessible at [[Elizabeth]] in the [[Community Center]], allows players to purchase exclusive, powerful items for [[Bits]]. Bits are only acquirable after consuming a [[Booster Cookie]].
## Items
Note that while this table can give a good estimate for the value of Bits Shop items, given the instability of [[Bazaar]] prices and the differences in supply and demand of the items themselves, items will likely be priced differently over time. Furthermore, the cost of [[Sacks]] rely on an estimate of [[Bits]] to [[Coins]] at each, as they cannot be sold on [[Bazaar]] nor the [[Auction House]].

# Brynmor
Categories: Pages with UIs, Shops, Abiphone NPCs, Non-player characters, Dwarven Mines
[Brynmor]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]): ✔ Yes
Description: Sells various [[Mining]]-related [[Consumables]].
Location: [[Dwarven Tavern]]
Coordinates: 32 202 -154.5
Brynmor is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Dwarven Tavern]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]]. They sell a variety of [[Consumables]] with various [[Mining]] buffs.
## Interface
Items only appear in the Shop once the player has met the [[Heart of the Mountain]] level requirement.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Brynmor: Welcome to the tavern!|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Brynmor: We're known best for our Powder Pie!|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Brynmor: Please enjoy yer stay!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) None|[NPC] Brynmor: ✆ Eh? My Contact? On my phone? [NPC] Brynmor: ✆ I s'pose you could have it, if you can help me out first. Select an option: [How can I help?]|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Selecting [How can I help?]|[NPC] Brynmor: ✆ One night, one of my regulars got a bit rowdy and took off towards the Abandoned Quarry. [NPC] Brynmor: ✆ He was carrying my most beloved antique - my Speckled Teacup. [NPC] Brynmor: ✆ By the time I caught up with him, he had already gone and DROPPED IT into the quarry's lake! [NPC] Brynmor: ✆ I fished about for a while, but didn't find nothin' apart from a bunch of grubbers. [NPC] Brynmor: ✆ If you ever have the time, could you go to the Abandoned Quarry and fish around for my Speckled Teacup? [NPC] Brynmor: ✆ If you manage to find it, bring it back here and I'll be happy to give you my contact. Select an option: [GIVE ITEM]|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Selecting [GIVE ITEM] without [[Speckled Teacup]]|[NPC] Brynmor: ✆ You don't have my beloved Speckled Teacup... [NPC] Brynmor: It's definitely still floating about in the waters of the Abandoned Quarry somewhere...|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Selecting [GIVE ITEM] with [[Speckled Teacup]]|[NPC] Brynmor: ✆ You found it! My Speckled Teacup! How amazing! [NPC] Brynmor: ✆ Lesson learned, I'm never letting anyone borrow it ever again - even my regulars! [NPC] Brynmor: ✆ Thanks for the help, here's my contact. ✆ Brynmor has neem added to your Abiphone's contacts!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Calling|[NPC] Brynmor: ✆ The usual?|

|Item|Effect|Price|Requirement|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Powder Pie]]|Grants +15% ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]] for 60 minutes.|50,000 [[Coins]]|Heart of the Mountain Tier 1 ([[Heart of the Mountain]])|
|[[Filet O' Fortune]]|Grants +20 [[Mining Fortune]] for 60 minutes.|100,000 [[Coins]]|Heart of the Mountain Tier 4 ([[Heart of the Mountain]])|

## Abiphone Contact
Brynmor's [[Abiphones|Abiphone]] contact can be obtained by giving them a [[Speckled Teacup]].
## Trivia
- Brynmor replaced Malmar, who appeared in various locations throughout the [[Dwarven Mines]] every 7th day of each SkyBlock month. He sold the [[Powder Pie|Mining Pumpkin]], with various buff changes.
- Brynmor is one of four NPCs mentioned during [[Cult of the Fallen Star]] Meeting #5, where Thondin states, "Brynmor always loses his [[Speckled Teacup]]" as one of several examples suggesting that every NPC lives in a simulation.
- The price of [[Packrat Shard]] and [[Fiesta Flask]] aren't affected by [[Shady Ring]] and its upgrades.

# Chocolate Factory
Aliases: Cf, Chocolate Shop, El Dorado, Golden Rabbit, Hitman, Hoppity's Collection, Rabbit Barn, Rabbit Hitman, Rabbit barn, Rabbit hitman, Stray, Stray Rabbit, Stray rabbit, Time Tower
Categories: Pages with UIs, Confirmations needed, More info needed, Templates with deprecated argument names
[Chocolate Factory]
The Chocolate Factory is a spiritual successor to [[Cookie Clicker]] found within [[Calendar|Calendar and Events]] or accessed via the /factory, /cf and /chocolatefactory commands.
## Mechanics
The Chocolate Factory is a minigame that consists of clicking to gain [[Chocolate]] and then spending it on upgrades and employees, as well as some items from the Chocolate Shop. Players can collect lifetime chocolate in order to unlock milestones and upgrade the level of their chocolate factory, allowing it to produce more Chocolate.
## Clicking
Players can click the Chocolate Factory icon in the UI to gain 1 [[Chocolate]]. This must be done at least 50 times to gain 50 [[Chocolate]], which can be used to hire [[Rabbit Bro]], in order to unlock automatic Chocolate production. Purchasing the Hand Baked Chocolate upgrade will increase the amount of Chocolate gained from clicking up to a maximum of 10 Chocolate at level 10.
### Stray Rabbits
Stray Rabbits have a 20% chance every 30 seconds to appear in the Chocolate Factory UI, staying for 15s, and will give [[Chocolate]] when clicked. Only rabbits the player has already found can appear (the exception being Fish the Rabbit and Golden Rabbits) and will give a different amount of [[Chocolate]] based on the rabbit's rarity and the player's current CpS.
A Stray Rabbit is guaranteed to appear at most 30 seconds after a [[Chocolate Eggs|Chocolate Egg]] has been collected (not including Side Dish). The first stray after collecting an egg has a chance to be a Golden Rabbit (10% by default, or 20% with Omega). These do not apply if the player swaps lobbies between collecting the egg and attempting to roll for a stray.
Chocolate granted from Stray Rabbits during [[Hoppity's Hunt]] will be doubled.

## Chocolate Factory Level
Once enough chocolate is accumulated in their current prestige, players are given the option to reset their progress in order to increase the level of their Chocolate Factory. This comes with many perks including applying a base Chocolate per Second multiplier, allowing rarer [[Chocolate Rabbits]] to be found, increasing the maximum amount of Chocolate that can be held at any one time and much more.

## Employees
There are 7 employees that can be purchased and upgraded, that being [[Rabbit Bro]], [[Rabbit Cousin]], [[Rabbit Sis]], [[Rabbit Daddy]], [[Rabbit Granny]], [[Rabbit Uncle]] and… [[Rabbit Dog]]. Each of these will spawn its [[NPC]] counterpart at the [[Rabbit House]] in the [[Hub Island|Hub]] once the previous employee reaches level 20. [[Rabbit Bro]], being the first, is always present
Each employee will automatically generate [[Chocolate]] every second and can be upgraded to produce more. The maximum level is 120 by default at Chocolate Factory Ⅰ. The table below details Level Caps.

### Employee Upgrade Cost
[[Rabbit Bro]]'s first 10 levels use the following cost formula, where multiplierLis the multiplier at level L.
upgrade_costL=(30+20×CF)×multiplierL

Level: Multiplier
[1]
[2]
3: 4
4: 6
5: 8
6: 9
7: 9.5
8: 10
9: 10.5

For all other cases, the cost to upgrade any employee to the next level can be calculated using the following formula, where i is the index of the employee in the order they can be unlocked (see the table below), CF is the player's Chocolate Factory level and L is the current employee level.
base_cost=(216+144×CF)×i2
upgrade_costL=base_cost×1.05L

### Discount
Unlocking Zeta Rabbit ([[Rarity|MYTHIC]]) reduces the cost of your employee upgrades by 1%. It is unlocked by reaching 250,000,000 [[Chocolate]] spent in the Chocolate Shop.
## Upgrades
Aside from employees, there are 5 total upgrades that each work in a unique way to help players max the Chocolate Factory. Same as employees, all upgrades are lost upon prestige.
### Rabbit Barn
The Rabbit Barn, unlocked at Chocolate Factory 1, determines the maximum number of unique [[Chocolate Rabbits]] the player can hold in their Hoppity's Collection. If the maximum number of Chocolate Rabbits is reached, then any subsequently obtained rabbits will be crushed.
This is the only upgrade that is not reset upon upgrading the Chocolate Factory.
Level 1 is unlocked by default, and the cost to unlock level 2 is 5,000 [[Chocolate]]. The cost for subsequent levels can be calculated using the following formula, where L is the desired rabbit barn level.
⌊5000×1.05L−1⌉
### Hand-Baked Chocolate
The Hand-Baked Chocolate upgrade, unlocked at Chocolate Factory 1, determines how much [[Chocolate]] is earnt by clicking. It can be upgraded to a maximum level of 10, giving +1 [[Chocolate]] per click per level for a maximum of 10 [[Chocolate]] per click.
This is the only way to increase the amount of Chocolate earnt per click in the Chocolate Factory.
The cost of each level can be calculated using the formula 500×(L−1), where L is the Hand-Baked Chocolate level. The cumulative cost at level 10 is 22,500 [[Chocolate]]. The cost is irrespective of the player's Chocolate Factory level.
### Time Tower
The Time Tower, unlocked at Chocolate Factory 2, allows the player to temporarily boost Chocolate production. The Time Tower slowly builds up to 3 charges, each over an 8 hour period, which can then be activated one at a time to add a multiplier to Chocolate production for 1 hour.
The Chocolate Rabbit Einstein decreases the charge time of the Time Tower to just 7h per charge or 21h for a full charge and Mu increases production by +0.7x [[Chocolate]].
The multiplier is equal to 110 times the level of the Time Tower, starting at +0.1x (+0.8x with Mu) at level 1 up to a maximum of +1.5x (+2.2x with Mu) at level 15.
Level 1 is unlocked by default. The cost for each subsequent level can be calculated using the following formula, where CF is the player's Chocolate Factory level and cost_multiplierL is a multiplier where L is the next level of the Time Tower according to the the table below.
upgrade_cost=(4,500,000+(500,000×CF))×cost_multiplierL

## Hoppity's Collection
Collecting unique [[Chocolate Rabbits]], through milestones, [[Hoppity]] or [[Chocolate Eggs|Chocolate Rabbit Eggs]], grants progress towards Hoppity's Collection and increase the player's chocolate per second. [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] Rabbits each grant a unique bonus, rather than the flat CpS boost that other rabbits give, and [[Rarity|DIVINE]] Rabbits each increase the maximum level of Employees by 1.
[[Rarity|MYTHIC]] Rabbits will give +5 CpS (base) when Sigma Rabbit is unlocked.
|Rarity|Chocolate per Second (base)|Chocolate per Second (multiplier)|
|-|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|+1|+0.002x|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|+2|+0.003x|
|RARE ([[Rarity]])|+4|+0.004x|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|+10|+0.005x|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])||+0.02x|
|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|+5||
|DIVINE ([[Rarity]])||+0.025x|
## Rabbit Hitman
If the player misses a [[Chocolate Eggs|Chocolate Egg]], the Rabbit Hitman can pick up the egg and store it to be claimed later. Eggs claimed this way can contain any unlocked [[Chocolate Rabbits|Chocolate Rabbit]], including Residents. Additionally, each Hitman Egg claimed can spawn a Golden Rabbit in the Chocolate Factory roughly 30 seconds after the next time the Chocolate Factory is opened.
Each egg takes up a slot in Hitman's storage. Once an egg is claimed, the slot goes on a cooldown of 20 minutes * AmountOfSlotsOnCooldown * (1 - ([[Attributes|Hitman Cooldown]] level * 0.025)), during which it cannot be reused to store another egg. Only one slot's cooldown is active at a time, so if the player had 2 slots on cooldown, the first one will be available after 20 minutes, and the second after 40 minutes.
Slots can be unlocked for a price in [[Coins]], increasing with each slot purchased. The total cost of purchasing all 28 slots is 292,111,000 [[Coins]]. Certain slots are locked until certain Chocolate Factory Level[More Info Needed]s.

## Maximum Chocolate Per Second Achievable
### Base Chocolate
- All Level 225 [[Rarity|DIVINE]] Employees (6,300 CpS)
- Completing Hoppity's Collection (1,514 CpS)
- Unlocking all 11 [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] Rabbits (55 CpS from [[Chocolate Rabbits|Sigma]])
- [[Prestige Chocolate Realm]] (50 CpS)
- [[Attributes|Rabbit Crew X]] Attribute (50 CpS)
- [[Attributes|Rabbit Youth X]] Attribute (10 Levels for Rabbit Bro, Cousin and Sis, 60 CpS)
- Golden Click Golden Rabbit (5 CpS until the end of [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Year]] per Golden Click Golden Rabbit found this year)
Max with zero Golden Click Golden Rabbits: 8,029 CpS.
### Multiplier
- Base Multiplier (1x Mult)
- Level 6 Chocolate Factory (1.5x Mult)
- Completing Hoppity's Collection (2.32x Mult)
- Level 20 Coach Jackrabbit (0.2x Mult)
- [[Booster Cookie]] (0.25x Mult)
- [[Hot Chocolate Mixin]] (0.05x Mult)
- [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Rabbit Pet]] (0.05x Mult)
- [[Attributes|Chocolate Speed X]] attribute (.025x Mult)
- Level 15 Time Tower with [[Chocolate Rabbits|Mu]] unlocked (2.2x Mult for an hour)
- [[Chocolate Rabbits|Chocobit]] (0.2x Mult until the end of the [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Year]] per Chocobit collected)
Max Mult with zero Chocobits: 5.395x Mult. With Time Tower enabled and zero Chocobits: 7.595x Mult
### Total Max Chocolate Production
Max Cps: 8,029 CpS * 5.395x = 43,316.455 [[Chocolate]] per second. With Time Tower enabled: 8,029 CpS * 7.595x = 60,980.255 [[Chocolate]] per second.
Averaging Time Tower being enabled 1/7th of the time: 43316.455 * 6/7 + 60980.255 * 1/7 = 45,839.855 [[Chocolate]] per second.
Which is 2.8M [[Chocolate]] per minute, 165M [[Chocolate]] per hour, or 4B [[Chocolate]] per day.

## Interface
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
This is how the UI will appear when the Chocolate Factory is fully upgraded.
On the [[Alpha Hypixel Network|Hypixel Alpha Network]], a button that gives 1,000,000,000 [[Chocolate]] is present for testing purposes.
## Tips
- Due to the Rabbit Hitman being able to spawn Golden Rabbits, it may be useful to claim Hitman Eggs one at a time.

# City Projects
Aliases: Bartender's Brewery, City Project, Community Project, Weaponsmith Workshop
Categories: Pages with UIs, Village, Community Center
City Projects are world events accessible from [[Elizabeth]] in the [[Community Center]]. Players are invited to contribute their resources to projects that will, upon their completion, cause updates to the game world (once development has finished). Players who contribute materials are rewarded with [[Fame Ranks|Fame]], and a special bonus related to the building upon its completion.
Players can contribute daily to each project. Projects require different contributions to complete.
## Projects
e [[City Projects/Hub Revamp|v]]
Special:FilePath/City Project (Hub Revamp).png

### Contributions
Requires a total of 400,000 contributions. The number of required contributions was increased various times from 100,000.

|Arrow Supplies|64x [[Enchanted Mithril]] 64x [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Stick]]|200|
|Labor|8x [[Enchanted Raw Mutton]] 64x [[Enchanted Melon Slice]] 200 [[Bits]]|1350|

# Collections
Aliases: Boss Collections, Collection, Unknown
Categories: Pages with UIs, Collections, Mechanics, SkyBlock Menu, Items, Common items
[Collections]
Collections are a system in [[SkyBlock]] where the player collects a certain resource to reach milestones and unlock rewards.
## Interfaces
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article! You may have visited: [[SkyBlock Menu/UI]]
## Mechanics
Collections are split into 7 different sections, with a total of 86 item Collections and 8 [[Bosses|BOSS]] Collections.
The player can view their Collections through the [[SkyBlock Menu]], or with /collections. Once the player has collected enough in a collection (5,000 for Fishing collections, 250 for Rift collections, and 25,000 for all other collections), they can see their global ranking for that Collection.
### Collection Items
Most Collections are progressed by collecting their corresponding Collection Item. Some Collection Items are the base form (like [[Rotten Flesh]]), which progress their Collections by 1. And some Collection Items are higher tiered forms (like [[Enchanted Rotten Flesh]] or [[Silver Magmafish]]) which progress their Collections by a higher amount, based on how many are needed to craft the higher tier form (like 160 for [[Enchanted Rotten Flesh]]).
Obtaining Collection Items manually or with [[Minions]] progresses the corresponding Collection, while Obtaining them from [[Shops|Merchants]], the [[Bazaar]], or through [[Trading]] does not progress the Collection.
### Boss Collections
Boss Collections are progressed by defeating the corresponding [[Bosses|BOSS]] instead of collecting items. The base form of a [[Bosses|BOSS]] progresses the Collection by 1, while harder forms of the [[Bosses|BOSS]] progress the Collection by increasing amounts.
### Rewards
Rewards are obtained after reaching certain milestones in a Collection. Rewards include [[Recipe Book|Recipes]], [[Trades]], Exp Discounts on [[Enchantments]], [[Skills|Skill Experience]], and [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock XP]]. Boss Collections also directly reward Items which can be claimed once.

# Composter
Aliases: Composter Upgrade, Composter Upgrades, Organic Matter, The Composter
Categories: Pages with UIs, Garden
[Composter]
The Composter is a feature in [[The Garden]] that allows the player to create [[Compost]]. The Composter takes 10 minutes to create each Compost, consuming 4000 Organic Matter and 2000 [[Machine Fuel]] per [[Compost]] continuously in the process. However, these values can be improved by upgrading the Composter.
## Mechanics
### Organic Matter
When inserted into the Composter, certain items can be converted into Organic Matter. Items cannot be taken out of the Composter once they have been inserted. 4000 Organic Matter is needed to make each Compost, excluding upgrades. The best item to buy from the [[Bazaar]] for making [[Composter|Organic Matter]] is currently [[Enchanted Seeds]] at 11,838 [[Coins]] per 4000 Organic Matter. On [[Ironman|♲ Ironman]] profiles, [[Enchanted Seeds]] and [[Box of Seeds]] are good because visitors don't ask for Seeds.
The following table gives the Organic Matter value for all known compostable items. All Organic Matter values below are exact. For example, [[Potato|Potatoes]] are worth exactly 0.33 organic matter, not 1/3. However, the amount per 4000 organic matter is rounded.
Note that a [[Condensed Fermento]] can only be used with sufficient Composter upgrades because they are worth more than the base limit of 40,000 Organic Matter.

### Upgrades
There are 5 Composter Upgrades available for the Composter.
- Composter Speed (requires [[The Garden|Garden II (2)]]): Increases Composter production speed by 20% per tier. The maximum speed is +500%, or 6x. Without the Multi Drop upgrade, each tier of this upgrade increases the Composter's production rate by 1.2 Compost per hour, worth about before accounting for Multi Drop.
- Multi Drop (requires [[The Garden|Garden IV (4)]]): When Compost is produced, grants a 3% chance per tier to produce an extra one. The maximum chance is 75%. Without the Composter Speed upgrade, each tier of this upgrade increases the Composter's production rate by 0.18 Compost per hour on average, worth about .
- Fuel Cap (requires [[The Garden|Garden VI (6)]]): Increases the maximum [[Machine Fuel]] that the Composter can hold by 30,000 per tier. Including the base value, the maximum limit is 850,000 [[Machine Fuel]].
- Organic Matter Cap (requires [[The Garden|Garden VIII (8)]]): Increases the maximum amount of Organic Matter that can be stored by 30,000 per tier. Including the base value, the maximum limit is 790,000 Organic Matter.
- Cost Reduction (requires [[The Garden|Garden X (10)]]): Reduces the amount of Organic Matter and [[Machine Fuel]] required to produce Compost by 1% per tier. The maximum reduction is 25%, which results in the consumption of 3,000 Organic Matter and 1,500 [[Machine Fuel]] per Compost.
A Composter with all Composter Upgrades maxed out would produce an average of 63 [[Compost]] per hour. For each hour, it would consume 108,000 Organic Matter and 54,000 [[Machine Fuel]]. It could run unattended for exactly 5 hours before running out of Organic Matter, making an average of 315 (4 stacks plus 59) [[Compost]] in that time. Even with the Organic Matter Cap upgrade maxed out, the first 6 upgrades of the Fuel Cap are enough for the Organic Matter to be depleted fully.
The table below gives the cost for each Composter Upgrade. The columns labeled Copper and Rare Crop Cost are the amount of [[Copper]] and Rare Crops needed to increase one of the upgrades to that tier. This cost is the same for each of the upgrades at that tier. The other columns are costs that only apply to the Composter Upgrade shown in the column heading.

The total raw material cost to max out all Composter Upgrades is given below. Excluding the [[Copper]], the raw materials would sell to [[Shops|Merchant]] NPCs for 905,685,520 [[Coins]], and the pre-crafted materials as listed in the total row of the table would cost at the [[Bazaar]].
- 178,250 [[Copper]]
- 945 (14 stacks plus 49) [[Cropie]]
- 945 [[Squash]]
- 735 (11 stacks plus 31) [[Fermento]]
- 12,124,160 (189,440 stacks) [[Wheat]]
- 22,681,600 (354,400 stacks) [[Carrot]]
- 23,321,600 (364,400 stacks) [[Potato]]
- 10,839,040 (169,360 stacks) [[Pumpkin]]
- 23,116,800 (361,200 stacks) [[Sugar Cane]]
- 33,280,000 (520,000 stacks) [[Melon Slice]]
- 12,083,200 (188,800 stacks) [[Cactus]]
- 33,920,000 (530,000 stacks) [[Cocoa Beans]]
- 11,166,720 (174,480 stacks) [[Mushroom|Brown Mushroom]]
- 8,652,800 (135,200 stacks) [[Mushroom|Red Mushroom]]
- 19,225,600 (300,400 stacks) [[Nether Wart]]
### SkyBlock XP Rewards
Upgrading the composter also grants SkyBlock Experience toward your overall SkyBlock Level. The XP is awarded based on the upgrade tier range:
- Tiers 1–7: +1 SkyBlock XP per tier
- Tiers 8–13: +2 SkyBlock XP per tier
- Tiers 14–19: +3 SkyBlock XP per tier
- Tiers 20–25: +4 SkyBlock XP per tier
These XP rewards contribute to the Composter Upgrades XP Task under [[SkyBlock Levels|Ways To Level Up]] in the SkyBlock Levels system.

# Crafted Minions
Aliases: Crafted Generators
Categories: Pages with UIs, Minions
Crafted Minions is a user interface where players can view minions and minion tiers they have crafted. To access it, follow these steps:
1. Open [[SkyBlock Menu]].
2. Open [[Collections]] menu.
3. Click on the minion head in the bottom portion of the menu.
It can also be accessed by doing /craftedgenerators or /craftedminions.
## Interface
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
## Features
- View the amount of your minion slots, unique minions crafted, and unique minions needed for the next minion slot.
- View the recipes for each minion.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Unknown Date|Added the Crafted Minions menu.|

# Critterdex
Aliases: Sparkling Critterdex
Categories: Pages with UIs
[Critterdex]
The Critterdex is a menu that documents the player's uniquely captured [[Critters|Critter]][[Critters|s]] in the [[Critter Safari]].
## UI
- This article is a work in progress.
- This article is incomplete and will be expanded on over the course of the article's creation.
- If you see missing, incorrect or out of date information, you are welcomed to edit this page.
This article was last edited by Pigicial's Pet Robot (contribs|logs|block) on 9:06, September 1 2026 (55 days ago)
## Overview
### Unlocking
The player can unlock the Critterdex from [["Hunter" Tobias]]. After Tobias denies the correlation of his father's ownership to his success, the player can either unlock the Critterdex by selecting [Nice!] or by replying with "sorry" after selecting [Suuuuuure...].
### Rewards
The player gains rewards for catching a critter for the first time, the rewards scale by the rarity of the critter. Critters captured by others and lootshared will not unlock the corresponding entry.
Rarity: Rewards
COMMON ([[Rarity]]): +2,500 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]]) 2,500 [[Coins]] 25 [[Safari Essence]]
UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]): +2,500 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]]) 2,500 [[Coins]] 50 [[Safari Essence]]
RARE ([[Rarity]]): +2,500 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]]) 2,500 [[Coins]] 75 (1 stack plus 11) [[Safari Essence]]
EPIC ([[Rarity]]): +2,500 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]]) 2,500 [[Coins]] 100 (1 stack plus 36) [[Safari Essence]]
LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]): +2,500 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]]) 2,500 [[Coins]] 150 (2 stacks plus 22) [[Safari Essence]]
### Sparkling Critterdex
Upon capturing all 37 [[Critters]] on the [[Critter Safari]], the player unlocks the Sparkling Critterdex, which tracks how many unique Sparkling Critters the player has captured. Capturing Sparkling Critters contributes to the player's Sparkling Milestones. Unlike the normal Critterdex, captures by other players will unlock the corresponding entries.
|Milestone|Requirements|Rewards|
|-|-|-|
|I|5 Sparkling Critters captured|Sparkling Critter Catcher Emblem ([[SkyBlock Levels]]) +10,000 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]]) 100 [[Safari Essence]]|
|II|10 Sparkling Critters captured|[[Sparkling Podium]] +20,000 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]]) 200 (3 stacks plus 8) [[Safari Essence]]|
|III|15 Sparkling Critters captured|[[Sparkling Helix Skin]] +30,000 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]]) 300 (4 stacks plus 44) [[Safari Essence]]|
|IV|20 Sparkling Critters captured|Gold Sparkling Critter Catcher Emblem ([[SkyBlock Levels]]) +40,000 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]]) 400 (6 stacks plus 16) [[Safari Essence]]|
|V|25 Sparkling Critters captured|[[Sparkling Ocelot Skin]] +50,000 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]]) 500 (7 stacks plus 52) [[Safari Essence]]|
|VI|30 Sparkling Critters captured|Safari Barn Skin ([[Barn Skins]]) +60,000 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]]) 600 (9 stacks plus 24) [[Safari Essence]]|
|VII|37 Sparkling Critters captured|Sparkling Critter Master Catcher Emblem ([[SkyBlock Levels]]) +70,000 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]]) 700 (10 stacks plus 60) [[Safari Essence]]|

# Drill Anvil
Aliases: Drill Mechanic, Drill mechanic
Categories: Pages with UIs, Drills
The Drill Anvil is a special kind of [[Anvil]] used to add [[Machine Fuel|Drill Fuel]] and [[Drills|Drill Parts]] to [[Drills]]. Against its description, it can still be used as a regular [[Anvil]]. Currently there are only 2 ways to access the Drill Anvil:
1. Talking to [[Jotraeline Greatforge]] in [[The Forge]] or calling with an [[Abiphones|Abiphone]].
2. Talking to [[Geonathan Greatforge]] in the [[Crystal Nucleus]].
Talking to [[Jotraeline Greatforge]] or [[Geonathan Greatforge]] requires a [[Drills|Drill]] in the player's inventory.
## Fuel
One of the main purposes of the Drill Anvil is to add fuel to [[Drills]] through any [[Machine Fuel]] source. Hovering over the [[Enchanted Cactus Green]] within the Drill Anvil interface displays the amount of fuel added for [[Flowers|Poppy]], [[Flowers|Dandelion]], [[Goblin Egg]], [[Biofuel]], [[Volta]], and [[Oil Barrel]]. These sources make up only 6 of the accepted 24 different [[Machine Fuel|Machine Fuels]].
## Installing and Removing Drill Parts
### Installing Drill Parts
The player can install a [[Drill Parts|Drill Part]] by placing a [[Drills|Drill]] on the left side of the anvil and a [[Drill Parts|Drill Part]] on the right side of the anvil. If the upgrade is possible, the hopper in the middle will begin to glow and the player will be able to view a preview of the upgrade. The upgrade can be confirmed by clicking on the hopper and taking the [[Drills|Drill]] from the anvil.
### Removing Drill Parts
The player can remove a [[Drill Parts|Drill Part]], by placing a [[Drills|Drill]] on the left side of the anvil. All currently added [[Drill Parts]] will appear in three slots in the left of the interface. Removing any [[Drill Parts|Drill Part]] costs 9,999 [[Coins]]. Clicking on any [[Drill Parts|Drill Part]] will remove it and return it to the player's inventory.

# Essence
Categories: Pages with UIs, Temp/Using Skill Aliases, Dungeons, Currencies, Items, Mob Drops
[Essence]
Essences are [[Currency|Currencies]] that can be used to upgrade items, and be spent at [[Essence Shops]].
## List of Essence Types
|Essence|Essence Shop|
|-|-|
|[[Wither Essence]]|Wither Essence Shop ([[Essence Shops]]) ( [[Malik]] in the [[Catacombs Entrance]] and [[Dungeon Hub]])|
|[[Undead Essence]]|Undead Essence Shop ([[Essence Shops]]) ( [[Malik]] in the [[Catacombs Entrance]] and [[Dungeon Hub]])|
|[[Crimson Essence]]|Crimson Essence Shop ([[Essence Shops]]) ( [[Vulcan]] in the [[Forgotten Skull]] and [[Dungeon Hub]])|
|[[Spider Essence]]|Spider Essence Shop ([[Essence Shops]]) ( [[Spider Tamer]] in [[Arachne's Sanctuary]])|
|[[Dragon Essence]]|Dragon Essence Shop ([[Essence Shops]]) ( [[Tyzzo]] in the [[Void Slate]])|
|[[Gold Essence]]|Gold Essence Shop ([[Essence Shops]]) ( [[Marigold]] in the [[Royal Mines]])|
|[[Diamond Essence]]|Diamond Essence Shop ([[Essence Shops]]) ( [[Gemma]] in the [[Crystal Nucleus]])|
|[[Ice Essence]]|Ice Essence Shop ([[Essence Shops]]) ( [[Frozen Alex]] by the [[Jerry Pond]])|
|[[Forest Essence]]|Forest Essence Shop ([[Essence Shops]]) ( [[Rosemary]] in the [[Murkwater Outpost]])|
|[[Fossil Essence]]|Fossil Essence Shop ([[Essence Shops]]) ( [[Researcher Beryl]] in the [[Dwarven Base Camp]])|
|[[Sun Gecko Essence]]|Sun Gecko Essence Shop ([[Oruo The Almighty]]) ([[Oruo The Almighty]] in the [[Continuum]])|
|[[Safari Essence]]|Safari Essence Shop ([[Essence Shops]]) ( [[Archie]] in the [[Critter Safari Entrance]] and the [[Critter Safari]])|
## Usage
### Upgrading and Dungeonizing Items
Essence can be used to upgrade and [[Dungeons|Dungeonize]] items. Each [[Star Upgrades|Star Upgrade]] grants a +2% stat boost. Dungeon Items grant a +10% stat boost per Star Upgrade while in the [[Catacombs]].
[[Dungeon Items]] can have a maximum of 5 Star Upgrades; Other items can have a maximum of up to 15 Star Upgrades.
A list of all items that can be upgraded by Essence are outlined below:
- [[Essence/List|Tab 1]]
### Essence Shops
Essence can be spent at several different [[Essence Shops]] for exclusive perks and items.
## Trivia
- [[Sun Gecko Essence]] is the only Essence not on the [[Bazaar]], due to it being [[Rift Dimension|Rift]]-exclusive.
- Purchasing most Essence from the [[Bazaar]] requires [[Catacombs|Catacombs XX (20)]], except for [[Forest Essence]] and [[Fossil Essence]].
- The maximum amount of Essence a player may have of one type is 2 billion.
